to deduce the estimate

\displaystyle k(\xi,\rho)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\mu}e^{-2\sqrt{\mu}|\rho|}\langle\rho\rangle\lesssim 1.

The remaining case
1<|\rho|
and
|\xi|<|\rho|
follows by reversing the role of
\xi
and
\rho
and use that
\langle\rho\rangle\lesssim\langle\rho-\xi\rangle+\langle\xi\rangle
to conclude.

For the proof of (
B.21
) and (
B.22
), we use Lemma 2.12 in
[
84
]
in the case
\sqrt{\mathrm{I}}(\mathrm{D})
is identity, then argue as above where
\sqrt{\mathrm{I}}(\mathrm{D})
can be treated as a zero order differential operator.
∎

B.2.
Estimates on pseudo-differential operators

In this section, we will give estimates of pseudo-differential operators whose symbol depends on the free surface. In particular, the framework needs to handle symbols of limited smoothness which is developed in
[
64
]
. To be precise, we give the definition of the objects we will study.

Definition B.6
.

Let
m\in{\mathbb{R}}
and
t_{0}>\frac{1}{2}
. A symbol
\sigma(x,\xi)
belongs to the class
\Gamma_{t_{0}}^{m}
if and only if

\sigma|_{{\mathbb{R}}\times\{|\xi|\leq 1\}}\in L^{\infty}(\{|\xi|\leq 1\}\>:\>H^{t_{0}}({\mathbb{R}}))

and for all
\beta\in{\mathbb{N}}
one has

\sup\limits_{|\xi|\geq\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{\beta-m}|\partial^{\beta}_{\xi}\sigma(\cdot,\xi)|_{H^{t_{0}}}<\infty.

We also introduce the seminorm

\mathcal{L}_{k,s}^{m}(\sigma):=\sup_{\beta\leq k}\>\sup_{|\xi|\geq\frac{1}{4}}\>\langle\xi\rangle^{\beta-m}|\partial_{\xi}^{\beta}\sigma(\cdot,\xi)|_{H^{s}},

Moreover, let
l_{k,s}
be a measure of the information in low frequencies given by

l_{s}(\sigma):=\sup_{\beta\leq k,|\xi|\leq 1}|\sigma(\cdot,\xi)|_{H^{s}},

and define

|\sigma|_{H^{s}_{(m)}}:=l_{s}(\sigma)+\mathcal{L}^{m}_{4,s}(\sigma).

The main tool we will use to derive our estimate is provided in
[
64
]
.

Proposition B.7
.

Let
f\in\mathscr{S}({\mathbb{R}})
,
t_{0}\in(\frac{1}{2},s_{0}]
. Then we have the following estimates:

1.

For
\sigma\in\Gamma_{s_{0}}^{m}
,
s\in(-t_{0},t_{0})
, and
m\in{\mathbb{R}}
, there holds,

(B.24)

|\mathrm{Op}(\sigma)f|_{H^{s}}\leq|\sigma|_{H^{t_{0}}_{(m)}}|f|_{H^{s+m}},

2.

Let
m_{1},m_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\sigma_{1}\in\Gamma_{s_{0}+\max\{m_{1},0\}+1}^{m_{1}}
and
\sigma_{2}\in\Gamma_{s_{0}+\max\{m_{2},0\}+1}^{m_{2}}
such that
-t_{0}<s+m_{j}\leq t_{0}+1
and
-t_{0}<s\leq t_{0}+1
.Then one has,

(B.25)

\displaystyle|[\mathrm{Op}(\sigma_{1}),\mathrm{Op}(\sigma_{2})]f|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\sigma^{1}|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(m_{1})}}|\sigma^{2}|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(m_{2})}}|f|_{H^{s+m_{1}+m_{2}-1}},

(B.26)

\displaystyle|\big(\mathrm{Op}(\sigma_{1})^{\ast}-\mathrm{Op}(\overline{\sigma}_{1})\big)f|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\sigma^{1}|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(m_{1})}}|f|_{H^{s+m_{1}-1}},

(B.27)

\displaystyle|\mathrm{Op}(\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2})-\mathrm{Op}(\sigma_{1})\circ\mathrm{Op}(\sigma_{2})f|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\sigma^{1}|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(m_{1})}}|\sigma^{2}|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(m_{2})}}|f|_{H^{s+m_{1}+m_{2}-1}}.

Remark B.8
.

The estimates provided in
[
64
]
hold for a larger class of symbols, but here, we only state the result for a symbol that is bounded in the origin and smooth for large frequencies. To be precise, estimate (
B.24
) follows from Theorem 1 in this paper and (
B.25
) is an application of Theorem 8. While estimate (
B.26
) and (
B.27
) are not given in
[
64
]
. However, as stated in footnote 8 and 9 of
[
65
]
, the composition estimate is used to prove Theorem 8, while the adjoint estimate can be deduced by similar methods.

Another important result is on Fourier multipliers that are merely bounded in low frequency.

Definition B.9
.

Let
m\in{\mathbb{R}}
and
t_{0}>\frac{1}{2}
. We say
\sigma\in\mathcal{M}^{m}
if and only if

\sigma^{1}\in L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})

and for all
\beta\in{\mathbb{N}}
one has

\sup\limits_{|\xi|\geq\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{\beta-m}|\partial^{\beta}_{\xi}\sigma^{1}(\xi)|<\infty.

In particular, we have from Theorem
6
(i)
[
64
]
:

Proposition B.10
.

Let
m_{1},m_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}
, and
\frac{1}{2}<t_{0}\leq s_{0}
. Let
\sigma^{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{m_{1}}
and
\sigma^{2}\in\Gamma^{m_{2}}_{s_{0}+\max\{m_{1},0\}+1}
. Then for all
s\in{\mathbb{R}}
such that
-t_{0}<s\leq t_{0}+1
and
-t_{0}<s+m_{1}\leq t_{0}+1
, there holds,

(B.28)

|[\sigma^{1}(\mathrm{D}),\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{2})]f|_{H^{s}}\lesssim\big(\sup\limits_{|\xi|\leq 1}|\sigma^{1}(\xi)|+\sup\limits_{\beta\leq 4|\xi|\geq\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{\beta-m_{1}}|\partial^{\beta}_{\xi}\sigma^{1}(\xi)|\big)|\sigma^{2}|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(m_{2})}}|f|_{H^{s+m_{1}+m_{2}-1}}.

Remark B.11
.

If we let
\sigma^{1}=\Lambda^{s}
and
s=\frac{1}{2}
then we have that

(B.29)

|[\Lambda^{s},\mathrm{Op}(\sigma)]f|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim|\sigma|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(m)}}|f|_{H^{s+m-\frac{1}{2}}}.

Lemma B.12
.

Let
t_{0}>\frac{1}{2}
,
s\geq 0
,
\zeta\in H^{t_{0}+3}({\mathbb{R}})
and define the symbol

L(x,\xi,z)=e^{-z\big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}{1+{\varepsilon}^{2}\mu(\partial_{x}\zeta)^{2}}-i\frac{{\varepsilon}\mu\partial_{x}\zeta\xi}{1+{\varepsilon}^{2}\mu(\partial_{x}\zeta)^{2}}\big)}.

Then for any
f\in\mathscr{S}({\mathbb{R}})
there is
C>0
nondecreasing function of its argument such that,

(B.30)

\displaystyle\|\Lambda^{s}\mathrm{Op}(L)\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}^{-})}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{-\frac{1}{4}}C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})|f|_{\mathring{H}^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}

(B.31)

\displaystyle\|\Lambda^{s}\mathrm{Op}(\partial_{z}L)f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}^{-})}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{\frac{1}{4}}C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})|f|_{\mathring{H}^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}

(B.32)

\displaystyle\|\Lambda^{s}\mathrm{Op}(\frac{1}{z}\partial_{x}L)f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}^{-})}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\mu^{\frac{3}{4}}C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{\mathring{H}^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}

(B.33)

\displaystyle\|\Lambda^{s}\mathrm{Op}(\frac{1}{z}\partial_{x}^{2}L)f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}^{-})}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\mu^{\frac{3}{4}}C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+3}})|f|_{\mathring{H}^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}

(B.34)

\displaystyle\|\Lambda^{s}\mathrm{Op}(\partial_{x}\partial_{z}L)f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}^{-})}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\mu^{\frac{3}{4}}C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{\mathring{H}^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}

(B.35)

\displaystyle\|\Lambda^{s}\mathrm{Op}(\partial_{x}L)\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}^{-})}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\mu^{\frac{1}{4}}C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{\mathring{H}^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}.

Remark B.13
.

Estimate (
B.31
) also holds for the symbol
\mathrm{Op}\big((\partial_{x}^{2}\zeta)\partial_{z}L\big)
, but with constant depending on
|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}}
.

Proof.

The inequalities are proved similarly, where we first consider the proof of (
B.30
). To employ Proposition
B.7
, we observe by Sobolev embedding that there exist
C=C(|\zeta|_{t_{0}+1})>0
nondecreasing such that

-\frac{1}{1+{\varepsilon}^{2}\mu(\partial_{x}\zeta)^{2}}\leq-\frac{1}{C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})},

and we use it to define the symbol:

\sigma_{z}(x,\xi)=e^{-z(\frac{1}{1+{\varepsilon}^{2}\mu(\partial_{x}\zeta)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2C})\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}e^{iz\frac{{\varepsilon}\mu\partial_{x}\zeta\xi}{1+{\varepsilon}^{2}\mu(\partial_{x}\zeta)^{2}}}.

Then for
z\in(0,\infty)
and any
|\xi|\geq\frac{1}{4}
we have that

\displaystyle|\partial_{\xi}^{\beta}\sigma_{z}(x,\xi)|_{H^{t_{0}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim C(|\partial_{x}\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}}})(z\sqrt{\mu})^{\beta}e^{-\frac{z}{2C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}

\displaystyle\leq C(|\partial_{x}\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}}})(z\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)^{\beta}e^{-\frac{z}{2C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}\langle\xi\rangle^{-\beta},

by the algebra property of
H^{t_{0}}({\mathbb{R}})
, so that

\mathcal{L}_{4,t_{0}}^{0}(\sigma_{z})\lesssim C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}).

While in low frequencies, the symbol is bounded, which implies

l_{0,t_{0}}(\sigma_{z})\leq C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}).

Since
\sigma\in\Gamma_{t_{0}}^{0}
we may use Proposition
B.7
with inequality (
B.24
) and Plancherel’s identity to say that

\displaystyle|\mathrm{Op}(L)f|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\leq C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})|\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{z}{2C}\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|}f|_{H^{s}}.

To conclude, we use this inequality with Plancherel’s identity and Fubini’s Theorem to see that

\displaystyle\|\Lambda^{s}\mathrm{Op}(L)\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}^{-})}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}|(\mathrm{Op}(L)\partial_{x}f)(\cdot,z)|_{H^{s}}^{2}\>\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\leq C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})\int_{0}^{\infty}|\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{z}{2C}\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|}\partial_{x}f|_{H^{s}}^{2}\>\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\leq C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}||\xi|^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle\xi\rangle^{s}\hat{f}(\xi)|^{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\frac{z}{C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}|\xi|\>\mathrm{d}z\mathrm{d}\xi

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})|f|_{\mathring{H}^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}.

The proof of the remaining inequalities essentially boils down to having one more polynomial power in
|\xi|
when compared to
z
. In particular, for the proof of (
B.31
), we see that there is a gain of
\sqrt{\mu}
and a
|\xi|
that appears after computing the derivative with respect to
z
. Therefore, the proof follows as it did for (
B.30
).

For the proof of (
B.32
), there is also a gain in the small parameters. However, there is a polynomial dependence in
z
and an additional
|\xi|
. Since we need
|\xi|
for the integrability, we define

\sigma_{z}^{1}(x,\xi)=|\xi|^{-1}\partial_{x}\sigma_{z}(x,\xi),

and make the computation for
|\xi|>\frac{1}{4}
:

\displaystyle|\partial_{\xi}^{\beta}\sigma_{z}^{1}(x,\xi)|_{H^{t_{0}}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})(z\mu)(z\sqrt{\mu})^{\beta}e^{-\frac{z}{2C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}

\displaystyle\leq z{\varepsilon}\mu C(|\partial_{x}\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})(z\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)^{\beta}e^{-\frac{z}{2C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}\langle\xi\rangle^{-\beta}.

Then we use Proposition
B.7
to find that

\displaystyle\|\Lambda^{s}\mathrm{Op}(\frac{1}{z}\partial_{x}L)f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}^{-})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq({\varepsilon}\mu)^{2}C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})\int_{0}^{\infty}|e^{-\frac{z}{2C}\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|}|\mathrm{D}|f|_{H^{s}}^{2}\>\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}^{2}\mu^{\frac{3}{2}}C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})|f|_{\mathring{H}^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}.

For the proof of (
B.33
), we argue similarly and define

\sigma_{z}^{2}(x,\xi)=|\xi|^{-1}\partial_{x}^{2}\sigma_{z}(x,\xi),

and find that

\displaystyle|\partial_{\xi}^{\beta}\sigma_{z}^{2}(x,\xi)|_{H^{t_{0}}}

\displaystyle\leq z{\varepsilon}\mu C(|\partial_{x}\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+3}})\langle\xi\rangle^{-\beta}.

At this point, the proof is the same as for (
B.32
).

For the proof of (
B.34
), we consider

\sigma_{z}^{3}(x,\xi)=|\xi|^{-1}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\sigma_{z}(x,\xi),

and finds that

\displaystyle|\partial_{\xi}^{\beta}\sigma_{z}^{3}(x,\xi)|_{H^{t_{0}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}C(|\partial_{x}\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}}})(z\mu)(\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)(z\sqrt{\mu})^{\beta}e^{-\frac{z}{2C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\mu C(|\partial_{x}\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}}})(z\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)^{\beta+1}e^{-\frac{z}{2C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}\langle\xi\rangle^{-\beta},

from which we deduce the final result as above.

For the proof of (
B.35
), we will apply the operator to
\partial_{x}f
so we consider

\sigma_{z}^{4}(x,\xi)=\partial_{x}\sigma_{z}(x,\xi),

and as for
\sigma_{z}^{1}
we find that

\displaystyle|\partial_{\xi}^{\beta}\sigma_{z}^{4}(x,\xi)|_{H^{t_{0}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}C(|\partial_{x}\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}}})(z\mu|\xi|)(z\sqrt{\mu})^{\beta}e^{-\frac{z}{2C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}C(|\partial_{x}\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}}})(z\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)^{\beta+1}e^{-\frac{z}{2C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}\langle\xi\rangle^{-\beta},

where there is a loss of
\mu^{\frac{1}{4}}
when computing the final integral as above.
∎

The next estimates are given on
H^{s}({\mathbb{R}})
and are simpler to deal with. They are versions of estimates used in
[
65
]
, but are listed here for the sake of clarity.

Proposition B.14
.

Let
{\varepsilon},\mu,\gamma,h_{\min}\in(0,1)
,
t_{0}>\frac{1}{2}
, and
\zeta\in H^{t_{0}+1}({\mathbb{R}})
such that

h:=1+\varepsilon\zeta\geq h_{\min},\quad\text{for all }x\in{\mathbb{R}}.

Then for
f,g\in\mathscr{S}({\mathbb{R}})
and
s\geq t_{0}+1
there exist
C=C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{s}})>0
nondecreasing function of its argument such that:

1.

Define the operator

\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}}\big)f(x)=-\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big(\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}t(x,\xi))\hat{f}(\xi)\Big)(x),

where

t(x,\xi)=(1+{\varepsilon}\zeta)\frac{\arctan({\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x}\zeta)}{{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x}\zeta}|\xi|.

Then for
s\in(-t_{0},t_{0})
there holds,

(B.36)

|\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}}\big)f|_{H^{s}}\leq C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})|f|_{H^{s}},

and for
s\in(-t_{0},t_{0}+1]
there holds,

(B.37)

\displaystyle|[\Lambda^{s},\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}}\big)]f|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}}

(B.38)

\displaystyle|(\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}}\big)^{\ast}-\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}}\big))f|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s-1}}.

2.

Define the operators

\mathrm{Op}\big(S_{J}\big)f(x)=\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big(\big(1+\gamma\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}t(x,\xi)\big)\hat{f}(\xi)\Big)(x)

\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)f(x)=\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big(\frac{\hat{f}(\xi)}{1+\gamma\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}t(x,\xi))}\Big)(x).

Then for
s\in(-t_{0},t_{0})
there holds,

(B.39)

|\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)f|_{H^{s}}\leq C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})|f|_{H^{s}},

and for
s\in(-t_{0},t_{0}+1]
there holds,

(B.40)

\displaystyle|\big(1-\mathrm{Op}(S_{J})\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)\big)f|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s-1}}

(B.41)

\displaystyle|\big[\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big),\mathcal{H}\big]f|_{H^{s}}\leq

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s-1}}

(B.42)

\displaystyle|\big[\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big),\partial_{x}\big]f|_{H^{s}}\leq

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s}}

(B.43)

\displaystyle|\big(\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)^{\ast}-\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)\big)f|_{H^{s}}\leq

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s-1}}.

3.

Define the operators

\displaystyle\mathrm{Op}\Big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}S_{J}}\Big)f(x)

\displaystyle=-\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big(\frac{\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}t(x,\xi))}{1+\gamma\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}t(x,\xi))}\hat{f}(\xi)\Big)(x)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Op}\Big(\partial_{x}\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}S_{J}}\Big)f(x)

\displaystyle=-\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big(\partial_{x}\frac{\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}t(x,\xi))}{1+\gamma\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}t(x,\xi))}\hat{f}(\xi)\Big)(x)

Then for
s\in(-t_{0},t_{0})
there holds,

(B.44)

\displaystyle|\mathrm{Op}\Big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}S_{J}}\Big)f|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\leq C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})|f|_{H^{s}},

(B.45)

\displaystyle|\mathrm{Op}\Big(\partial_{x}\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}S_{J}}\Big)f|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s}}.

and for
s\in(-t_{0},t_{0}+1]
there holds,

(B.46)

\displaystyle|[\mathrm{Op}\Big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}S_{J}}\Big),f]\partial_{x}g|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s}}|g|_{H^{s}}

(B.47)

\displaystyle|\Big(\mathrm{Op}\Big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}S_{J}}\Big)^{\ast}-\mathrm{Op}\Big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}S_{J}}\Big)\Big)f|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s-1}}

(B.48)

\displaystyle|\Big(\mathrm{Op}\Big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}}\Big)\mathrm{Op}\Big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\Big)-\mathrm{Op}\Big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}S_{J}}\Big)\Big)f|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s-1}}

4.

Define the operators

\mathrm{Op}\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{P}^{2}}{S_{J}S^{-}}\Big)f(x)=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu}}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big(\frac{|\xi|(1+\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)^{-1}}{1+\gamma\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}t(x,\xi))}\hat{f}(\xi)\Big)(x)

\mathrm{Op}\Big(\frac{S_{J}S^{-}}{\mathfrak{P}^{2}}\Big)\mathfrak{P}f(x)=-\sqrt{\mu}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big(\frac{1+\gamma\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}t(x,\xi))}{|\xi|(1+\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)^{-1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{P}f}(\xi)\Big)(x).

Then for
k=0,1,
and
s\in(-t_{0},t_{0})
there holds,

(B.49)

\displaystyle|\big(\mathrm{Op}(S^{-}S_{J})-\mathrm{Op}(S^{-})\mathrm{Op}(S_{J})\big)f|_{H^{s+\frac{k}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\mu^{1-\frac{k}{4}}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{\dot{H}_{\mu}^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}.

(B.50)

\displaystyle|\big(1-\mathrm{Op}(\frac{\mathfrak{P}^{2}}{S_{J}S^{-}})\mathrm{Op}(\frac{S_{J}S^{-}}{\mathfrak{P}^{2}})\big)\mathfrak{P}f|_{H^{s+\frac{k}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\mu^{-\frac{k}{4}}C(|\zeta|_{H^{{t_{0}+2}}})|f|_{H^{s}}.

Proof.

We consider each point separately in individual steps.

Step 1.
For the proof of (
B.36
), we will verify that

(B.51)

\sigma:=\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}}=\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}t)\in\Gamma_{t_{0}}^{0}.

In this case, the proof follows by Theorem
B.7
and estimate (
B.24
):

\displaystyle|\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}}\big)f|_{H^{s}}\leq|\sigma|_{H^{t_{0}}_{(0)}}|f|_{H^{s}}.

To verify (
B.51
), we observe by Sobolev embedding and the non-cavitation condition that

\displaystyle h_{\min}\leq h=1+\varepsilon\zeta\leq C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}}}),

and

\displaystyle 0<\frac{1}{C(|\zeta|_{t_{0}+1})}\leq g:=\frac{\arctan(\varepsilon\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x}\zeta)}{\varepsilon\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x}\zeta}\leq C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}).

Then since
|\tanh(r)|\leq|r|
we can use it to prove that
\sigma
satisfies

|\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}t(\cdot,\xi))|_{H^{t_{0}}}\leq C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}),

for all
\xi\in{\mathbb{R}}
. On the other hand, for
|\xi|\geq\frac{1}{4}
and
\beta\geq 1
we have that
\xi\mapsto\sigma(\cdot,\xi)
is smooth and since

(B.52)

r\mapsto\tanh(r)=1-\frac{2}{e^{2r}+1},

we have that

\displaystyle|\partial_{\xi}^{\beta}\sigma(\cdot,\xi)|_{H^{t_{0}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})(\sqrt{\mu})^{\beta}e^{-\frac{1}{C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}

\displaystyle\leq C(|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}})(\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)^{\beta}e^{-\frac{1}{C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}\langle\xi\rangle^{-\beta},

which implies

|\sigma|_{H^{t_{0}}_{(0)}}\leq C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{t_{0}+1}).

For the proof of (
B.37
), we will use (
B.29
). However, to make the small parameters appear, we decompose
\sigma
into three pieces:

\displaystyle\>\sigma(x,\xi)

\displaystyle=\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|h(x)g(x))

\displaystyle=\Big(\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|h(x)g(x))-\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|g(x))\Big)+\Big(\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|g(x))-\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)

(B.53)

\displaystyle=\sigma^{1}(x,\xi)+\sigma^{2}(x,\xi)+\sigma^{3}(\xi),

where
h
and
g
are as above. Then for
\sigma(x,\xi)=\Sigma(h(x),g(x),\xi)
, we have
(h,g)\mapsto\Sigma(h,g,\xi)
is smooth and for

\sigma^{1}(x,\xi)=\Sigma(h(x),g(x),\xi)-\Sigma(1,g(x),\xi),

we observe by the Mean Value Theorem, the algebra property of
H^{t_{0}+1}({\mathbb{R}})
, and the identity

|h-1|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}={\varepsilon}|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}

that

\displaystyle|\sigma^{1}(\cdot,\xi)|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|e^{-\frac{1}{C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|},

for any
\xi\in{\mathbb{R}}
. While for
|\xi|\geq\frac{1}{4}
we note that

|\partial_{\xi}^{\beta}\partial_{h}\Sigma(h(\cdot),f(\cdot),\xi)|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}\lesssim C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})(\sqrt{\mu})^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|e^{-\frac{1}{C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|},

to get

\displaystyle|\partial_{\xi}^{\beta}\sigma^{1}(\cdot,\xi)|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})(\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)^{\beta+1}e^{-\frac{1}{C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}\langle\xi\rangle^{-\beta},

and we conclude that

|\sigma^{1}|_{H^{t_{0}}_{(0)}}\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{t_{0}+2}).

For the estimate on
\sigma^{2}
, we argue similarly but instead note that
|\frac{\arctan(r)}{r}-1|\leq|r|
and we can use it to obtain

|g-1|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}\leq{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}|\partial_{x}\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}},

from which we deduce the bound

|\sigma^{2}|_{H^{t_{0}}_{(0)}}\leq{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}}).

Then since
[\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}},\sigma^{3}(\mathrm{D})]f=0
, we may conclude by (
B.29
) that

\displaystyle|[\Lambda^{s},\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}}\big)]f|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq|[\Lambda^{s},\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{1})]f|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}+|[\Lambda^{s},\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{2})]f|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}}.

For the proof of (
B.38
), we again use the decomposition given by (
B.53
) and then conclude by (
B.26
) using that the symbol is real-valued and
(\sigma^{3})^{\ast}=\sigma^{3}
.

Step 2.
For the proof of (
B.39
), we argue as in the previous step to find that

(B.54)

\frac{1}{1+\gamma\sigma}\in\Gamma_{t_{0}}^{0}\quad\text{with}\quad\Big|\frac{1}{1+\gamma\sigma}\Big|_{H^{t_{0}}_{(0)}}\leq C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}),

Then for (
B.39
) we use (
B.24
).

For the proof of (
B.40
) we use the notation in Step 1. to make the following decomposition

\displaystyle 1-\mathrm{Op}(S_{J})\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)

\displaystyle=\gamma\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{\sigma}{S_{J}}\big)-\gamma\mathrm{Op}(\sigma)\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)

\displaystyle=\gamma\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\Big(\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{\sigma^{j}}{S_{J}}\big)-\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{j})\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)\Big)-\gamma[\sigma^{3}(\mathrm{D}),\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Op}(A_{1})+\mathrm{Op}(A_{2}).

where we used that
\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)\circ\sigma^{3}(\mathrm{D})=\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{\sigma^{3}}{S_{J}}\big)
. Then to estimate the contribution of
A_{1}
, we apply the composition estimate (
B.27
), the estimates in Step 1., and (
B.54
) to deduce that

\displaystyle|\mathrm{Op}(A_{1})f|_{H^{s}}=\Big|\Big(\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{\sigma^{j}}{S_{J}}\big)-\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{j})\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)\Big)f\Big|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\sigma^{j}|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(0)}}\big|\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(0)}}|f|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s-1}},

for
j=1,2
. On the other hand, for the symbol associated to
A_{2}
we observe that

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{1}{S_{J}}-\frac{1}{1+\gamma\sigma^{3}(\mathrm{D})}\Big|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(0)}}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\big|\frac{\sigma^{1}}{(1+\gamma\sigma)(1+\gamma\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|g))}\big|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(0)}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\gamma\Big|\frac{\sigma^{2}}{(1+\gamma\sigma)(1+\gamma\tanh(\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|))}\Big|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(0)}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\gamma C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}}),

and then conclude by the commutator estimate (
B.28
) that

\displaystyle|\mathrm{Op}(A_{2})f|_{H^{s}}=|[\sigma^{3}(\mathrm{D}),\mathrm{Op}\big(\frac{1}{S_{J}}\big)]f|_{H^{s}}\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|f|_{H^{s-1}}.

For the proof of (
B.41
), (
B.42
), and (
B.43
), we argue as above where we use (
B.28
) for the first two, and for (
B.43
) we use the adjoint estimate (
B.26
).

Step 3.
For the proof of estimates (
B.44
), (
B.45
), (
B.46
), (
B.47
), and (
B.48
) we simply use the observation

\gamma\frac{S^{+}}{S^{-}S_{J}}=\frac{1}{1+\gamma\sigma}-1,

and arguing similarly as in Step 2. to deduce the results.

For the proof of (
B.49
), we use the notation in Step 1. to make the observation that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Op}(S^{-}S_{J})-\mathrm{Op}(S^{-})\mathrm{Op}(S_{J})

\displaystyle=\gamma\sqrt{\mu}\Big(\mathrm{Op}(|\mathrm{D}|\sigma)-|\mathrm{D}|\mathrm{Op}(\sigma)\Big)

\displaystyle=\gamma\sqrt{\mu}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\Big(\mathrm{Op}(|\mathrm{D}|\frac{\sigma^{j}}{\mathfrak{P}})-|\mathrm{D}|\mathrm{Op}(\frac{\sigma^{j}}{\mathfrak{P}})\Big)\mathfrak{P}

\displaystyle=-\gamma\sqrt{\mu}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}[|\mathrm{D}|,\mathrm{Op}(\frac{\sigma^{j}}{\mathfrak{P}})\Big)]\mathfrak{P}.

Then we would like to conclude by the commutator estimate (
B.28
):

\displaystyle|\big(\mathrm{Op}(S^{-}S_{J})-\mathrm{Op}(S^{-})\mathrm{Op}(S_{J})\big)f|_{H^{s+\frac{k}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\mu}\max_{j=1,2}\Big|\big[|\mathrm{D}|,\mathrm{Op}(\frac{\sigma^{j}}{\mathfrak{P}})\big]\mathfrak{P}f\Big|_{H^{s+\frac{k}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\mu}\max_{j=1,2}|\frac{\sigma^{j}}{\mathfrak{P}}|_{H^{t_{0}+1}_{(-\frac{k}{2})}}|\mathfrak{P}f|_{H^{s}}.

Then, arguing as in Step 1. we deduce that

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{P}^{-1}\sigma^{j}(\cdot,\xi)|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})(1+\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\frac{1}{C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}}),

and for
|\xi|\geq\frac{1}{4}
we get

\displaystyle|\partial_{\xi}^{\beta}(\mathfrak{P}^{-1}\sigma^{j}(\cdot,\xi))|_{H^{t_{0}+1}}

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})(1+\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|)^{\frac{1}{2}}(\sqrt{\mu})^{\beta}e^{-\frac{1}{C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|},

\displaystyle\leq{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{\frac{k}{2}}\langle\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|\rangle^{\beta+\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\frac{1}{C}\sqrt{\mu}|\xi|}\langle\xi\rangle^{-\beta-\frac{k}{2}}.

We conclude the final estimate from the following bound

|\mathfrak{P}^{-1}\sigma^{j}|_{H^{t_{0}}_{(-\frac{k}{2})}}\leq{\varepsilon}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{k}{4}}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}}).

Step 4.
For the proof of (
B.50
), we need to work on the domain of
\mathfrak{P}f
for the composition to be well-defined in low frequency. With this in mind, we define the symbols

\sigma^{4}=\frac{\mathfrak{P}^{2}}{S_{J}S^{-}}\quad\text{and}\quad\sigma^{5}=\frac{S_{J}S^{-}}{\mathfrak{P}^{2}},

then make the decomposition

\displaystyle\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{4})\circ\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{5})\circ|\mathrm{D}|

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Op}(\frac{1}{S_{J}})\circ\frac{|\mathrm{D}|}{(1+\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|)}\circ\mathrm{Op}(S_{J})\circ(1+\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Op}(\frac{1}{S_{J}})\circ|\mathrm{D}|\circ\mathrm{Op}(S_{J})+\mathrm{Op}(\frac{1}{S_{J}})\circ\frac{|\mathrm{D}|}{(1+\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|)}\circ[\mathrm{Op}(S_{J}),\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Op}(\frac{1}{S_{J}})\circ\mathrm{Op}(S_{J})\circ|\mathrm{D}|+\gamma\mathrm{Op}(\frac{1}{S_{J}})\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}[|\mathrm{D}|,\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{j})]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\gamma\mathrm{Op}(\frac{1}{S_{J}})\circ\frac{\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|}{(1+\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|)}\circ\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}[\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{j}),|\mathrm{D}|],

and use it together with the boundedness of
\mathrm{Op}(\frac{1}{S_{J}})
, given by (
B.39
), to see that

\displaystyle\mathrm{LHS}_{\eqref{1 - op b2...}}

\displaystyle=|\big(1-\mathrm{Op}(\frac{\mathfrak{P}^{2}}{S_{J}S^{-}})\mathrm{Op}(\frac{S_{J}S^{-}}{\mathfrak{P}^{2}})\big)\mathfrak{P}f|_{H^{s+\frac{k}{2}}}

\displaystyle=\Big|\big(1-\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{4})\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{5})\big)|\mathrm{D}|\frac{\mathfrak{P}}{|\mathrm{D}|}f\Big|_{H^{s+\frac{k}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\big(1-\mathrm{Op}(\frac{1}{S_{J}})\circ\mathrm{Op}(S_{J})\big)\mathfrak{P}f\Big|_{H^{s+\frac{k}{2}}}+\max\limits_{j=1,2}\Big|[|\mathrm{D}|,\mathrm{Op}(\sigma^{j})]\frac{\mathfrak{P}}{|\mathrm{D}|}f\Big|_{H^{s+\frac{k}{2}}}.

Then by (
B.40
) and the commutator estimate (
B.28
) we conclude that

\mathrm{LHS}_{\eqref{1 - op b2...}}\leq{\varepsilon}C(h_{\min}^{-1},|\zeta|_{H^{t_{0}+2}})|(1+\sqrt{\mu}|\mathrm{D}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}}f|_{H^{s+\frac{k}{2}}}.

∎
